DTC Description | DTC

B12F0 Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with Electric Steering Lock

Is DTC B12F0 indicated?

YES

Go to step 2.

NO

Intermittent failure, the system is OK at this time. Check for loose or poor connections.

- Open wire check (+B SMART line) -1. Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. -2. Disconnect the following connector. Electric steering lock 12P connector -3. Measure the voltage between test points 1 and 2. Test condition Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode Electric steering lock 12P connector: disconnected Test point 1 Electric steering lock 12P connector No. 3 Test point 2 Body ground Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC. Is there battery voltage? YES The +B SMART wire is OK. Go to step 3. NO Repair an open or high resistance in the wire.

-1. Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

-2. Disconnect the following connector.

Electric steering lock 12P connector

-3. Measure the voltage between test points 1 and 2.

Test condition | Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode Electric steering lock 12P connector: disconnected

Test point 1 | Electric steering lock 12P connector No. 3

Test point 2 | Body ground

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Is there battery voltage?

YES

The +B SMART wire is OK. Go to step 3.

NO

Repair an open or high resistance in the wire.

- Open wire check (GND line) -1. Check for continuity between test points 1 and 2. Test condition Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode Electric steering lock 12P connector: disconnected Test point 1 Electric steering lock 12P connector No. 7 Test point 2 Body ground Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC. Is there continuity? YES The GND wire is OK. Go to step 4. NO Repair an open or high resistance in the ground wire or poor ground (G505).

-1. Check for continuity between test points 1 and 2.

Test condition | Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode Electric steering lock 12P connector: disconnected

Test point 1 | Electric steering lock 12P connector No. 7

Test point 2 | Body ground

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Is there continuity?

YES

The GND wire is OK. Go to step 4.

NO

Repair an open or high resistance in the ground wire or poor ground (G505).

- Shorted wire check (IGN TRX line) -1. Disconnect the following connector. Body control module connector G (32P) -2. Check for continuity between test points 1 and 2. Test condition Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode Electric steering lock 12P connector: disconnected Body control module connector G (32P): disconnected Test point 1 Electric steering lock 12P connector No. 6 Test point 2 Body ground Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC. Is there continuity? YES Repair a short to ground in the wire. NO The IGN TRX wire is not shorted. Go to step 5.

-1. Disconnect the following connector.

Body control module connector G (32P)

-2. Check for continuity between test points 1 and 2.

Test condition | Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode Electric steering lock 12P connector: disconnected Body control module connector G (32P): disconnected

Test point 1 | Electric steering lock 12P connector No. 6

Test point 2 | Body ground

Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC.

Is there continuity?

YES

Repair a short to ground in the wire.

NO

The IGN TRX wire is not shorted.

# DTC U0100, U0122 (Keyless Access Power Control Unit with CAN gateway)

DTC U0100 : Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with PCM

DTC U0122: Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with VSA Modulator-Control Unit (VSA Message)

NOTE:

- Before you troubleshoot, review the general troubleshooting information .

- According to the detected DTC(s), check for the power circuit and the ground circuit of the control unit which cannot communicate with the body control module.

- Refer to the DTC shown on the display, then inspect the connectors and terminals based on the instructions.

DTC Description | DTC

U0100 Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with PCM

U0122 Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with VSA Modulator-Control Unit (VSA Message)

DTC (Power Control Unit)

- CAN gateway system DTC check -1. Turn the vehicle to the ON mode. -2. Check for CAN gateway system DTCs with the HDS. DTC Description DTC U0029-00 CAN Gateway F-CAN ch A Bus Off U0047-00 CAN Gateway F-CAN ch B Bus Off U3000-49 CAN Gateway Internal Failure DTC (CAN GATEWAY) Is DTC U0029-00, U0047-00, and/or U3000-49 indicated? YES Go to the troubleshooting for CAN gateway system DTC(s) . NO Go to step 2.

-1. Turn the vehicle to the ON mode.

-2. Check for CAN gateway system DTCs with the HDS.

DTC Description | DTC

U0029-00 CAN Gateway F-CAN ch A Bus Off

U0047-00 CAN Gateway F-CAN ch B Bus Off

U3000-49 CAN Gateway Internal Failure

DTC (CAN GATEWAY)

Is DTC U0029-00, U0047-00, and/or U3000-49 indicated?

YES

Go to the troubleshooting for CAN gateway system DTC(s) .

NO

Go to step 2.

- F-CAN circuit communication check -1. Select the FUNCTION TEST in the CAN gateway with the HDS, then select the F-CAN Bus Connected Unit Check. F-CAN Bus Connected Unit Check Does the body control module detect the CAN gateway Bus channels A? Bus A is Not Available Go to step 3. Bus A is Available Go to step 4.

-1. Select the FUNCTION TEST in the CAN gateway with the HDS, then select the F-CAN Bus Connected Unit Check.

F-CAN Bus Connected Unit Check

Does the body control module detect the CAN gateway Bus channels A?

Bus A is Not Available

Go to step 3.

Bus A is Available

Go to step 4.

- Open wire check (F-CAN A lines) -1. Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. -2. Disconnect the following connectors. CAN gateway 12P connector *1 CAN gateway 16P connector *2 Body control module connector B (36P) *1: CAN gateway 12P connector type *2: CAN gateway 16P connector type -3. Check for continuity between test points 1 and 2 respectively. CAN gateway 12P connector type Test condition Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode CAN gateway 12P connector: disconnected Body control module connector B (36P): disconnected Test point 1 CAN gateway 12P connector No. 3 Test point 2 Body control module connector B (36P) No. 22 Test point 1 CAN gateway 12P connector No. 9 Test point 2 Body control module connector B (36P) No. 23 Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC. CAN gateway 16P connector type Test condition Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode CAN gateway 16P connector: disconnected Body control module connector B (36P): disconnected Test point 1 CAN gateway 16P connector No. 3 Test point 2 Body control module connector B (36P) No. 22 Test point 1 CAN gateway 16P connector No. 11 Test point 2 Body control module connector B (36P) No. 23 Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC. Is there 1.0 Ω or less? YES The F-CAN A_H and F-CAN A_L wires are OK. Check for poor connections or loose terminals at the CAN gateway and the body control module. Substitute a known-good body control module , then recheck. If the DTC U0100 goes away and the body control module was substituted, replace the original body control module . NO Repair an open in F-CAN A_H or F-CAN A_L wire between the CAN gateway and the body control module.

-1. Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

-2. Disconnect the following connectors.

CAN gateway 12P connector *1 CAN gateway 16P connector *2 Body control module connector B (36P)

*1: CAN gateway 12P connector type

*2: CAN gateway 16P connector type

-3. Check for continuity between test points 1 and 2 respectively.

Test condition | Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode CAN gateway 12P connector: disconnected Body control module connector B (36P): disconnected

Test point 1 | CAN gateway 12P connector No. 3

Test point 2 | Body control module connector B (36P) No. 22

# DTC U0100, U0122 (Keyless Access Power Control Unit without CAN gateway)

DTC U0100 : Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with PCM

DTC U0122: Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with VSA Modulator-Control Unit (VSA Message)

NOTE:

- Before you troubleshoot, review the general troubleshooting information .

- According to the detected DTC(s), check for the power circuit and the ground circuit of the control unit which cannot communicate with the body control module.

- Refer to the DTC shown on the display, then inspect the connectors and terminals based on the instructions.

DTC Description | DTC

U0100 Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with PCM

U0122 Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with VSA Modulator-Control Unit (VSA Message)

DTC (Power Control Unit)

- Problem verification -1. Turn the vehicle to the ON mode. -2. Clear the DTCs with the HDS. -3. Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode and then the ON mode. -4. Wait for at least 6 seconds. -5. Check for DTCs with the HDS. DTC Description DTC U0100 Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with PCM U0122 Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with VSA Modulator-Control Unit (VSA Message) Are any DTCs indicated? YES Go to step 2. NO Intermittent failure, the system is OK at this time. Check for poor connections or loose terminals at the transmitting control units and the gauge control module.

-1. Turn the vehicle to the ON mode.

-2. Clear the DTCs with the HDS.

-3. Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode and then the ON mode.

-4. Wait for at least 6 seconds.

-5. Check for DTCs with the HDS.

DTC Description | DTC

U0100 Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with PCM

U0122 Power Control Unit (Body Control Module) Lost Communication with VSA Modulator-Control Unit (VSA Message)

Are any DTCs indicated?

YES

Go to step 2.

NO

Intermittent failure, the system is OK at this time. Check for poor connections or loose terminals at the transmitting control units and the gauge control module.

U0100: Jump the SCS line with the HDS, and wait more than 1 minute. SCS short -4. Disconnect the transmitting control unit connector(s). Refer to the DTC shown on the display, then disconnect the connector(s) based on the instructions (see table). DTC Connector U0100 PCM connector A (50P) *1/*3 PCM connector No. 2 (58P) *2 U0122 VSA modulator-control unit 46P connector *1: L15B7/L15BA/L15BY engine *2: K20C1 engine *3: K20C2 engine -5. Check for continuity between the receiving control unit and the transmitting control unit on the F-CAN_H circuit and the F-CAN_L circuit. Test condition Receiving control unit connector(s): disconnected Transmitting control unit connector(s) for indicated DTCs: disconnected DTC Circuit name Receiving control unit Transmitting control unit Connector Terminal Connector Terminal U0100 *1/*3 F-CAN_H Body control module connector B (36P) No. 22 PCM connector A (50P) No. 37 F-CAN_L No. 23 No. 36 U0100 *2 F-CAN_H Body control module connector B (36P) No. 22 PCM connector No. 2 (58P) No. 15 F-CAN_L No. 23 No. 27 U0122 F-CAN_H Body control module connector B (36P) No. 22 VSA modulator-control unit 46P connector No. 20 F-CAN_L No. 23 No. 21 *1: L15B7/L15BA/L15BY engine *2: K20C1 engine *3: K20C2 engine Is there continuity? YES The F-CAN_H wire and the F-CAN_L wire are OK. Refer to the DTC shown on the display, substitute or replace the control unit based on the instructions (see table). -4. Check for continuity between the receiving control unit and the transmitting control unit on the B-CAN_H circuit and the B-CAN_L circuit.

Test condition | Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode Body control module connector B (36P): disconnected Transmitting control unit connector for indicated DTCs: disconnected

DTC | Circuit name | Receiving control unit | Transmitting control unit

Connector | Terminal | Connector | Terminal

U128D | B-CAN_H | Body control module connector B (36P) | No. 20 | Gauge control module connector A (32P) | No. 30

B-CAN_L | No. 21 | No. 31

Is there continuity?

YES

The B-CAN_H wire and the B-CAN_L wire are OK.

Refer to the DTC shown on the display, then substitute or replace the control unit based on the instructions (see table).

DTC | Operation for transmitting control unit

U128D | Substitute a known-good gauge control module , then recheck.

# Keyless/Power Door Locks/Security System Symptom Troubleshooting - AUTO DOOR UNLOCK does not work (4-door)

NOTE:

- Before testing, make sure the No. A1-5*1, No. A2-2, and No. A18 fuses are OK.

- Check if Auto Door Unlock feature selected OFF, under MID customized settings for each remote transmitter: Driver 1 and 2. Make sure to test operation of each Auto Door Unlock setting to check function.

Make sure to test operation of each Auto Door Unlock setting to check function.

- Before testing, make sure the No. B8, No. B9, No. B11*2, No. B12, No. B13, No. B15, No. B16, No. B25, No. B26, No. B28, No. B38, and No. B39 fuses are OK.

- There are two pairs of fuses in the same circuit (No. B25 and No. B39 fuses, No. B12 and No. B26 fuses, No. B13 and No. B38 fuses). If one fuse is blown, make sure to check the other fuse in the same circuit. If necessary, replace the blown fuse(s). *1: With keyless access system *2: Except L15BY engine

*1: With keyless access system

*2: Except L15BY engine

- Abnormal circuit check 1 -1. Connect the HDS to the data link connector (DLC) . -2. Select DOOR LOCKS from the BODY ELECTRICAL SYSTEM SELECT menu with the HDS, and enter FUNCTIONAL TESTS. BODY ELECTRICAL - DOOR LOCKS - Functional Tests - Inspection -3. Select UNLOCK ALL DOORS with the HDS. Do the door lock actuators work normally? YES Go to step 34. NO: Driver's door lock circuit (Except L15BY engine) Go to step 2. NO: Driver's door lock circuit (L15BY engine) Go to step 4. NO: Front passenger's door lock circuit (Except L15BY engine) Go to step 10. NO: Front passenger's door lock circuit (L15BY engine) Go to step 12. NO: Left rear door lock circuit (Except L15BY engine) Go to step 18. NO: Left rear door lock circuit (L15BY engine) Go to step 20. NO: Right rear door lock circuit (Except L15BY engine) Go to step 26. NO: Right rear door lock circuit (L15BY engine) Go to step 28.

-1. Connect the HDS to the data link connector (DLC) .

-2. Select DOOR LOCKS from the BODY ELECTRICAL SYSTEM SELECT menu with the HDS, and enter FUNCTIONAL TESTS.

BODY ELECTRICAL - DOOR LOCKS - Functional Tests - Inspection

-3. Select UNLOCK ALL DOORS with the HDS.

Do the door lock actuators work normally?

YES

Go to step 34.

NO: Driver's door lock circuit (Except L15BY engine)

Go to step 2.

NO: Driver's door lock circuit (L15BY engine)

Go to step 4.

NO: Front passenger's door lock circuit (Except L15BY engine)

Go to step 10.

NO: Front passenger's door lock circuit (L15BY engine)

Go to step 12.

NO: Left rear door lock circuit (Except L15BY engine)

Go to step 18.

NO: Left rear door lock circuit (L15BY engine)

Go to step 20.

NO: Right rear door lock circuit (Except L15BY engine)

Go to step 26.

NO: Right rear door lock circuit (L15BY engine)

Go to step 28.

- Open wire check (+B F/BOX MAIN2 line) -1. Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode. -2. Measure the voltage between test points 1 and 2. Test condition Vehicle OFF (LOCK) mode Test point 1 Under-dash fuse/relay box connector C (27P) No. 3 Test point 2 Body ground Courtesy of HONDA, U.S.A., INC. Is there battery voltage? YES The +B F/BOX MAIN2 wire is OK. Go to step 3. NO Repair an open or high resistance in the wire.

-1. Turn the vehicle to the OFF (LOCK) mode.

-2. Measure the voltage between test points 1 and 2.

YES

Is there continuity? YES The GND wire is OK. Go to step 6. NO Repair an open or high resistance in the ground wire or poor ground (G501).
